// dist/admin/editorial-admin.types.d.ts
import type { createCmsSdkClient } from "@trinacria-cms/sdk";
import type { EntryBody } from "../modules/entries/structured-document.contract.js";
export type CmsClient = ReturnType<typeof createCmsSdkClient>;
export interface ApiEnvelope<T> {
    data: T;
}
export type ContentFieldType = "text" | "rich_text" | "number" | "boolean" | "date_time" | "select" | "url" | "media" | "relation" | "json" | "repeatable";
export declare const CONTENT_MODEL_ICON_OPTIONS: readonly [{
    readonly value: "file-text";
    readonly label: "Documento";
}, {
    readonly value: "file";
    readonly label: "Pagina";
}, {
    readonly value: "calendar-days";
    readonly label: "Evento";
}, {
    readonly value: "image";
    readonly label: "Galleria";
}, {
    readonly value: "globe";
    readonly label: "Sito";
}, {
    readonly value: "package";
    readonly label: "Scheda";
}, {
    readonly value: "sparkles";
    readonly label: "In evidenza";
}];
export interface ContentTypeField {
    key: string;
    label: string;
    type: ContentFieldType;
    required: boolean;
    multiple: boolean;
    helpText?: string;
    config?: {
        options?: readonly string[];
        [key: string]: unknown;
    };
}
export interface ContentWorkflowState {
    key: string;
    label: string;
    initial: boolean;
}
export interface ContentWorkflowTransition {
    key: string;
    label: string;
    from: string;
    to: string;
    requiredPermission?: "submit" | "review" | "approve" | "publish";
}
export interface ContentWorkflow {
    preset: "review" | "direct" | "custom";
    name?: string;
    states: readonly ContentWorkflowState[];
    transitions: readonly ContentWorkflowTransition[];
}
export interface EditorialContentType {
    id: string;
    key: string;
    name: string;
    description?: string;
    icon?: string;
    status: "active" | "archived";
    fields: readonly ContentTypeField[];
    taxonomyIds: readonly string[];
    workflowId?: string;
    workflow?: ContentWorkflow;
    updatedAt: string;
    deletedAt?: string;
    delivery?: {
        enabled: boolean;
        publicFields: readonly string[];
        exposeTitle: boolean;
        exposeBody: boolean;
        exposeSlug: boolean;
    };
}
export interface EditorialEntryRecord {
    id: string;
    contentTypeId: string;
    ownerUserId: string;
    reviewerUserId?: string;
    title?: string;
    slug?: string;
    body?: EntryBody;
    data: Record<string, unknown>;
    status: string;
    scheduledAt?: string;
    publishedAt?: string;
    version?: number;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export type EditorialNavigator = (routeId: string, params?: URLSearchParams) => void;

        modelId: string;
    };
}[]>;

// dist/modules/content-types/repositories/content-types.repository.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { CreateContentTypeInput, UpdateContentTypeInput } from "../content-types.input.js";
import { type ContentTypeRecord } from "../content-types.schemas.js";
export declare class ContentTypesRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private scope?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter);
    create(input: CreateContentTypeInput & {
        createdByUserId: string;
    }): Promise<ContentTypeRecord>;
    findById(id: string, options?: {
        includeDeleted?: boolean;
    }): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    findByKey(key: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    list(options?: {
        status?: ContentTypeRecord["status"];
        deleted?: boolean;
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly ({
        key: string;
        name: string;
        id: string;
        status: "active" | "archived";
        fields: ({
            key: string;
            label: string;
            type: "number" | "boolean" | "text" | "rich_text" | "date_time" | "select" | "url" | "media" | "relation" | "json" | "repeatable";
            required: boolean;
            multiple: boolean;
        } & {
            helpText?: string | undefined;
            config?: ({} & {
                options?: string[] | undefined;
                targetContentTypeId?: string | undefined;
                allowedMimeTypes?: string[] | undefined;
            }) | undefined;
        })[];
        taxonomyIds: string[];
        ownershipScope: "inherit" | "own_entries" | "all_entries";
        version: number;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
    } & {
        description?: string | undefined;
        icon?: string | undefined;
        workflowId?: string | undefined;
        workflow?: ({
            preset: "review" | "direct" | "custom";
            states: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                initial: boolean;
            } & {})[];
            transitions: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                from: string;
                to: string;
            } & {
                requiredPermission?: "submit" | "review" | "approve" | "publish" | undefined;
            })[];
        } & {
            name?: string | undefined;
        }) | undefined;
        showInMainNavigation?: boolean | undefined;
        delivery?: ({
            enabled: boolean;
            publicFields: string[];
            exposeTitle: boolean;
            exposeBody: boolean;
            exposeSlug: boolean;
        } & {}) | undefined;
        deliveryConfigVersion?: number | undefined;
        deletedAt?: string | undefined;
    })[]>;
    update(id: string, input: UpdateContentTypeInput, version?: number): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    softDelete(id: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    restore(id: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    hardDelete(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    fence(id: string, version: number): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    private repository;
}

// dist/modules/content-types/services/content-types.service.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { OperationAuthorizer, OperationContext, PluginEventPublisher } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/contracts";
import type { MongoDurableEventStore } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime";
import { type CreateContentTypeInput, type UpdateContentTypeInput } from "../content-types.input.js";
import type { ContentTypeRecord } from "../content-types.schemas.js";
import { ContentTypesRepository } from "../repositories/content-types.repository.js";
export declare class ContentTypeValidationError extends Error {
    readonly code: string;
    constructor(message: string);
}
export declare class ContentTypeConflictError extends ContentTypeValidationError {
    readonly code = "conflict";
}
export declare class ContentTypesService {
    private readonly repository;
    private readonly db?;
    private readonly sessionDb?;
    private readonly authorization?;
    private readonly durable?;
    private readonly publisher?;
    constructor(repository: ContentTypesRepository, db?: DbAdapter | undefined, sessionDb?: DbAdapter | undefined, authorization?: {
        context: OperationContext;
        authorizer: OperationAuthorizer;
    } | undefined, durable?: MongoDurableEventStore | undefined, publisher?: PluginEventPublisher | undefined);
    withAuthorization(context: OperationContext, authorizer: OperationAuthorizer): ContentTypesService;
    private assertAction;
    createContentType(input: CreateContentTypeInput, createdByUserId: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord>;
    /** Creates the usable blog baseline once, without overwriting local changes. */
    ensureDefaultContentTypes(): Promise<void>;
    getContentTypeForEntry(id: string, fence?: boolean): Promise<({
        key: string;
        name: string;
        id: string;
        status: "active" | "archived";
        fields: ({
            key: string;
            label: string;
            type: "number" | "boolean" | "text" | "rich_text" | "date_time" | "select" | "url" | "media" | "relation" | "json" | "repeatable";
            required: boolean;
            multiple: boolean;
        } & {
            helpText?: string | undefined;
            config?: ({} & {
                options?: string[] | undefined;
                targetContentTypeId?: string | undefined;
                allowedMimeTypes?: string[] | undefined;
            }) | undefined;
        })[];
        taxonomyIds: string[];
        ownershipScope: "inherit" | "own_entries" | "all_entries";
        version: number;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
    } & {
        description?: string | undefined;
        icon?: string | undefined;
        workflowId?: string | undefined;
        workflow?: ({
            preset: "review" | "direct" | "custom";
            states: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                initial: boolean;
            } & {})[];
            transitions: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                from: string;
                to: string;
            } & {
                requiredPermission?: "submit" | "review" | "approve" | "publish" | undefined;
            })[];
        } & {
            name?: string | undefined;
        }) | undefined;
        showInMainNavigation?: boolean | undefined;
        delivery?: ({
            enabled: boolean;
            publicFields: string[];
            exposeTitle: boolean;
            exposeBody: boolean;
            exposeSlug: boolean;
        } & {}) | undefined;
        deliveryConfigVersion?: number | undefined;
        deletedAt?: string | undefined;
    }) | null>;
    getContentType(id: string): Promise<({
        key: string;
        name: string;
        id: string;
        status: "active" | "archived";
        fields: ({
            key: string;
            label: string;
            type: "number" | "boolean" | "text" | "rich_text" | "date_time" | "select" | "url" | "media" | "relation" | "json" | "repeatable";
            required: boolean;
            multiple: boolean;
        } & {
            helpText?: string | undefined;
            config?: ({} & {
                options?: string[] | undefined;
                targetContentTypeId?: string | undefined;

            }) | undefined;
        })[];
        taxonomyIds: string[];
        ownershipScope: "inherit" | "own_entries" | "all_entries";
        version: number;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
    } & {
        description?: string | undefined;
        icon?: string | undefined;
        workflowId?: string | undefined;
        workflow?: ({
            preset: "review" | "direct" | "custom";
            states: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                initial: boolean;
            } & {})[];
            transitions: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                from: string;
                to: string;
            } & {
                requiredPermission?: "submit" | "review" | "approve" | "publish" | undefined;
            })[];
        } & {
            name?: string | undefined;
        }) | undefined;
        showInMainNavigation?: boolean | undefined;
        delivery?: ({
            enabled: boolean;
            publicFields: string[];
            exposeTitle: boolean;
            exposeBody: boolean;
            exposeSlug: boolean;
        } & {}) | undefined;
        deliveryConfigVersion?: number | undefined;
        deletedAt?: string | undefined;
    }) | null>;
    listContentTypes(options?: {
        status?: ContentTypeRecord["status"];
        deleted?: boolean;
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly ({
        key: string;
        name: string;
        id: string;
        status: "active" | "archived";
        fields: ({
            key: string;
            label: string;
            type: "number" | "boolean" | "text" | "rich_text" | "date_time" | "select" | "url" | "media" | "relation" | "json" | "repeatable";
            required: boolean;
            multiple: boolean;
        } & {
            helpText?: string | undefined;
            config?: ({} & {
                options?: string[] | undefined;
                targetContentTypeId?: string | undefined;
                allowedMimeTypes?: string[] | undefined;
            }) | undefined;
        })[];
        taxonomyIds: string[];
        ownershipScope: "inherit" | "own_entries" | "all_entries";
        version: number;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
    } & {
        description?: string | undefined;
        icon?: string | undefined;
        workflowId?: string | undefined;
        workflow?: ({
            preset: "review" | "direct" | "custom";
            states: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                initial: boolean;
            } & {})[];
            transitions: ({
                key: string;
                label: string;
                from: string;
                to: string;
            } & {
                requiredPermission?: "submit" | "review" | "approve" | "publish" | undefined;
            })[];
        } & {
            name?: string | undefined;
        }) | undefined;
        showInMainNavigation?: boolean | undefined;
        delivery?: ({
            enabled: boolean;
            publicFields: string[];
            exposeTitle: boolean;
            exposeBody: boolean;
            exposeSlug: boolean;
        } & {}) | undefined;
        deliveryConfigVersion?: number | undefined;
        deletedAt?: string | undefined;
    })[]>;
    updateContentType(id: string, input: UpdateContentTypeInput): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    deleteContentType(id: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    restoreContentType(id: string): Promise<ContentTypeRecord | null>;
    permanentlyDeleteContentType(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    private assertDeliveryFields;
    private atomic;
    private hasEntries;
    private assertEmpty;
    private assertFieldsAreValid;
    private assertWorkflowIsValid;
    private ensureDefaultContentType;
}

export declare class EntryValidationError extends Error {
    readonly code: string;
    constructor(message: string);
}
export declare class EntryConflictError extends EntryValidationError {
    readonly code = "conflict";
}
export declare class EntryAccessError extends Error {
    readonly code = "auth_forbidden_admin_required";
    constructor(message?: string);
}
export type EditorialTransition = string;
export interface EditorialDomainEventPublisher {

}
export interface EntryAccessScope {

    canUseAuthorScope?: boolean;
}

// dist/modules/entries/structured-document.contract.d.ts
/**
 * Framework-independent types and helpers for the persisted editorial document.
 * They are safe to import from both the server and the browser.
 */
export type BlockColor = "gray" | "brown" | "orange" | "yellow" | "green" | "blue" | "purple" | "pink" | "red";
export interface BlockAppearance {
    textColor?: BlockColor;
    backgroundColor?: BlockColor;
}
/** Inline formatting is stored as data, never as executable or arbitrary HTML. */
export interface InlineText {
    text: string;
    bold?: boolean;
    italic?: boolean;
    code?: boolean;
    /** A validated absolute URL or an editorial entry reference. */
    link?: {
        href: string;
        entryId?: string;
    };
}
export interface RichTextData {
    text: string;
    /** Optional for backwards compatibility with V1 plain-text documents. */
    inline?: InlineText[];
}
export interface ParagraphBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "paragraph";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: RichTextData;
}
export interface HeadingBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "heading";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: RichTextData & {
        level: 2 | 3 | 4;
    };
}
export interface QuoteBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "quote";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: RichTextData & {
        citation?: string;
    };
}
export interface ImageBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "image";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: {
        src: string;
        alt: string;
        assetId?: string;
        caption?: string;
    };
}
export interface ListBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "list";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: {
        style: "bulleted" | "numbered";
        items: string[];
    };
}
export interface TableBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "table";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: {
        rows: string[][];
        hasHeader: boolean;
    };
}
export interface LayoutColumn {
    id: string;
    blocks: StructuredContentBlock[];
}
export interface LayoutBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "layout";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: {
        columns: LayoutColumn[];
    };
}
export interface DividerBlock {
    id: string;
    type: "divider";
    version: 1;
    appearance?: BlockAppearance;
    data: Record<string, never>;
}
export type StructuredContentBlock = ParagraphBlock | HeadingBlock | QuoteBlock | ImageBlock | ListBlock | TableBlock | LayoutBlock | DividerBlock;
export type StructuredContentBlockType = StructuredContentBlock["type"];
export interface StructuredDocument {
    version: 1;
    blocks: StructuredContentBlock[];
}
/** Kept read-compatible while existing plain-text entries are progressively saved again. */
export interface LegacyTextBody {
    text: string;
}
export type EntryBody = StructuredDocument | LegacyTextBody;
export declare function createEmptyStructuredDocument(): StructuredDocument;
export declare function createStructuredContentBlock(type: StructuredContentBlockType, id: string): StructuredContentBlock;
/** Converts a legacy `{ text }` value in memory; the stored value changes only on save. */
export declare function toStructuredDocument(body?: EntryBody): StructuredDocument;

// dist/modules/entries/structured-document.operations.d.ts
import type { StructuredDocument } from "./structured-document.contract.js";
/** Reorders a root-level block using an insertion point in the original document. */
export declare function moveStructuredContentBlock(document: StructuredDocument, blockId: string, insertionIndex: number): StructuredDocument;
/** Moves a block between the root canvas and any layout column, preserving the document tree. */
export declare function moveStructuredContentBlockToContainer(document: StructuredDocument, blockId: string, containerId: string, insertionIndex: number): StructuredDocument;

// dist/modules/revisions/revisions.repository.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { type EntryRevisionRecord } from "./revisions.schemas.js";
export declare class RevisionsRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private scope?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter);
    create(revision: EntryRevisionRecord): Promise<EntryRevisionRecord>;
    findById(entryId: string, id: string): Promise<EntryRevisionRecord | null>;
    listByEntryId(entryId: string, options?: {
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly ({
        id: string;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        entryId: string;
        revisionNumber: number;
        reason: string;
        snapshotJson: string;
    } & {})[]>;
    private repository;
}

// dist/modules/revisions/revisions.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
export declare const EntryRevisionRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdByUserId: string;
    createdAt: string;
    entryId: string;
    revisionNumber: number;
    reason: string;
    snapshotJson: string;
} & {}>;
export type EntryRevisionRecord = Infer<typeof EntryRevisionRecordSchema>;
/** Revisions are append-only records, kept outside the working entry document. */
export declare const ENTRY_REVISIONS_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
        id: string;
        createdByUserId: string;
        createdAt: string;
        entryId: string;
        revisionNumber: number;
        reason: string;
        snapshotJson: string;
    } & {}>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};
